{"forms":[{"form":"amphitheatres","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"amphitheater","tags":["alternative","US"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Ancient Roman amphitheatres were mostly oval or circular in plan, with seating tiers that surrounded the central performance area, like a modern open-air stadium.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An open, outdoor theatre (which may be a theatre in the round, or have a stage with seating on only one side), especially one from the classical period of ancient Greece or Rome, or a modern venue of similar design."]},{"glosses":["A natural formation of a similar shape, where a steep mountain or slope or particular rock formation forms a partial or complete bowl, especially one used as a performance space (and possibly modified by carving out seats, etc) because the slopes naturally amplify or echo sound."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈæmfɪˌθiːətə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈæmfɪˌθɪətə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈæmfiˌθiːətə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈæm.pəˌθi.(j)ə.tɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈæm.fəˌθi.(j)ə.tɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":260706,"upstream_offset":1298260169,"upstream_record_sha256":"a87a2ba271232c74a481bd891bf385ace7d533db3cb01a643a2942b7fdde0402","word":"amphitheatre"}
